Definitions: Mathlib.CategoryTheory, part 24 of 25

Mathlib.CategoryTheory.Square

CategoryTheory.Functor.mapSquaredefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Square, function or constant
Wording
the map square of F (from the name)
English
Let $F$ be a functor of $C$ and $D$. Then the map square of $F$ is a functor of the set of squares of $C$ and the set of squares of $D$.
Formula
$F \in \operatorname{Functor} (C , D)\vdash \operatorname{mapSquare} (F)\in \operatorname{Functor} (\operatorname{Square} (C), \operatorname{Square} (D))$
French
Soit $F$ un foncteur de $C$ et $D$. Alors le carré d'applications de $F$ est un foncteur de l'ensemble de carrés de $C$ et l'ensemble de carrés de $D$.
CategoryTheory.NatTrans.mapSquaredefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Square, function or constant
Wording
the map square of x2 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{2}$ be a homomorphism of $F$ and $G$. Then the map square of $x_{2}$ is a homomorphism of the map square of $F$ and the map square of $G$.
Formula
$x_{2} \in \operatorname{Hom} (F , G)\vdash \operatorname{mapSquare} (x_{2})\in \operatorname{Hom} (\operatorname{mapSquare} (F), \operatorname{mapSquare} (G))$
French
Soit $x_{2}$ un homomorphisme de $F$ et $G$. Alors le carré d'applications de $x_{2}$ est un homomorphisme du carré d'applications de $F$ et le carré d'applications de $G$.
CategoryTheory.SquarestructureMathlib.CategoryTheory.Square, type
Wording
square of C (from the name)
English
Let $C$ be a set. Then the squares of $C$ are a basic type.
Formula
$C \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{Square} (C)\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soit $C$ un ensemble. Alors les carrés de $C$ sont un type de base.
CategoryTheory.Square.HomstructureMathlib.CategoryTheory.Square, type
Wording
homomorphism of x1 and x2 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ be squares of $C$. Then the homomorphisms of $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$ are a basic type.
Formula
$x_{1} , x_{2} \in \operatorname{Square} (C)\vdash \operatorname{Hom} (x_{1} , x_{2})\ \mathsf{set}$
French
Soient $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ des carrés de $C$. Alors les homomorphismes de $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$ sont un type de base.
CategoryTheory.Square.Hom.compdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Square, function or constant
Wording
the composition of f and g (from the name)
English
Let $f$ be a homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $x_{2}$. Let $g$ be a homomorphism of $x_{2}$ and $x_{3}$. Then the composition of $f$ and $g$ is a homomorphism of $x_{1}$ and $x_{3}$.
Formula
$f \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{1} , x_{2}), g \in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{2} , x_{3})\vdash \operatorname{comp} (f , g)\in \operatorname{Hom} (x_{1} , x_{3})$
French
Soit $f$ un homomorphisme de $x_{1}$ et $x_{2}$. Soit $g$ un homomorphisme de $x_{2}$ et $x_{3}$. Alors la composition de $f$ et $g$ est un homomorphisme de $x_{1}$ et $x_{3}$.
CategoryTheory.Square.Hom.iddefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Square, function or constant
Wording
the identity of sq (from the docstring)
English
Let $\mathrm{sq}$ be a square of $C$. Then the identity of $\mathrm{sq}$ is a homomorphism of $\mathrm{sq}$ and $\mathrm{sq}$.
Formula
$\mathrm{sq} \in \operatorname{Square} (C)\vdash \operatorname{id} (\mathrm{sq})\in \operatorname{Hom} (\mathrm{sq} , \mathrm{sq})$
French
Soit $\mathrm{sq}$ un carré de $C$. Alors l'identité de $\mathrm{sq}$ est un homomorphisme de $\mathrm{sq}$ et $\mathrm{sq}$.

Wording

English

Formula
$\operatorname{arrowArrowEquivalence} \in \operatorname{Equivalence} (\operatorname{Square} (C), \operatorname{Arrow} (\operatorname{Arrow} (C)))$
French
L'équivalence est une équivalence de l'ensemble de carrés de $C$ et l'ensemble de flèches de l'ensemble de flèches de $C$.

English

Formula

CategoryTheory.Square.isoMkdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Square, function or constant
Wording
the isomorphism of x5 and x6 (from the name)
English
Let $x_{3}$ be an isomorphism of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_X\_u2081\_}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_X\_u2081\_}$ applied to $x_{2}$. Let $x_{4}$ be an isomorphism of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_X\_u2082\_}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_X\_u2082\_}$ applied to $x_{2}$. Let $x_{5}$ be an isomorphism of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_X\_u2083\_}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_X\_u2083\_}$ applied to $x_{2}$. Let $x_{6}$ be an isomorphism of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_X\_u2084\_}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_X\_u2084\_}$ applied to $x_{2}$. Assume that the composition of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_f\_u2081\_\_u2082\_}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and the homomorphism of $x_{4}$ is equal to the composition of the homomorphism of $x_{3}$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_f\_u2081\_\_u2082\_}$ applied to $x_{2}$. Assume that the composition of $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_f\_u2081\_\_u2083\_}$ applied to $x_{1}$ and the homomorphism of $x_{5}$ is equal to the composition of the homomorphism of $x_{3}$ and $\mathrm{CategoryTheory\_\_Square\_\_f\_u2081\_\_u2083\_}$ applied to $x_{2}$. Soit $G$ un sous-foncteur de $F$. Soit $x_{2}$ une fonction des éléments $i$ de $x_{1}$ à des éléments de l'action de $F$ et $X$ appliqué à $i$. Alors $\operatorname{IsGeneratedBy}$ tient pour $G$, $i$ et $x_{2}$ si le supremum de la fonction qui envoie $i$ au sous-foncteur de $i$ appliqué à $i$ est égal à $G$.

Let $C$ be a set. Then the presheaf of $C$ is a functor of the set of opposites of $C$ and the set of sets.

$C \in \mathrm{Set} \vdash \operatorname{presheaf} (C)\in \operatorname{Functor} (\mathrm{Opposite} (C), \mathrm{Set})$
French
Soit $C$ un ensemble. Alors le préfaisceau de $C$ est un foncteur de l'ensemble d'opposés de $C$ et l'ensemble d'ensembles.

Mathlib.CategoryTheory.Subterminal

CategoryTheory.IsSubterminaldefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Subterminal, predicate
Wording
A is subterminal (from the name)
English
Let $A$ be an element of $C$. Then $A$ is subterminal, if for all elements $Z$ of $C$, for all homomorphisms $f$ and $g$ of $Z$ and $A$, $\EqtMACRoH {f}{g}$.
Formula
$A \in C \vdash \operatorname{IsSubterminal} (A):\Leftrightarrow \forall Z \in C . \forall f \in \operatorname{Hom} (Z , A). \forall g \in \operatorname{Hom} (Z , A). \EqtMACRoJ {f}{g}$
French
Soit $A$ un élément de $C$. Alors $A$ est sous-terminal si pour tous les éléments $Z$ de $C$, pour tous les homomorphismes $f$ et $g$ de $Z$ et $A$, $\EqtMACRoH {f}{g}$.
CategoryTheory.IsSubterminal.isoDiagdefMathlib.CategoryTheory.Subterminal, function or constant
Wording
the isomorphism diagonal of hA (from the name)
English
Assume that $A$ is subterminal. Then the isomorphism diagonal of $\mathrm{hA}$ is an isomorphism of the product of $A$ and $A$ and $A$ defined as the inverse of $\operatorname{asIso}$ applied to the diagonal of $A$.
Formula
$\operatorname{IsSubterminal} (A)\vdash \operatorname{isoDiag} (\mathrm{hA}):= \operatorname{symm} (\operatorname{asIso} (\operatorname{diag} (A)))\in \operatorname{Iso} (\operatorname{prod} (A , A), A)$
French

the kernel of F (from the docstring)
